{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"clams","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A bivalve mollusk of many kinds, especially those that are edible; for example soft-shell clams (Mya arenaria), hard clams (Mercenaria mercenaria), sea clams or hen clams (Spisula solidissima), and other species, possibly originally applied to clams of species Tridacna gigas, a huge East Indian bivalve."]},{"glosses":["A type of strong pincers or forceps."],"tags":["historical","in-plural"]},{"glosses":["A kind of vise, usually of wood."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Those sneakers cost me fifty clams!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A dollar."],"tags":["US","in-plural","slang"]},{"glosses":["A Scientologist."],"tags":["derogatory","slang"]},{"glosses":["A vagina or vulva."],"tags":["slang","vulgar"]},{"glosses":["A wrong or misplaced note."],"tags":["slang"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["One who clams up; a taciturn person, one who refuses to speak."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["mouth (Now found mostly in the expression shut one's clam)"],"tags":["US","dated","slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/klæm/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[kleəm] ~ [klɛəm]"},{"ipa":"/klam/","tags":["British","Northern-England","Scotland","Southern","Standard","Wales"]},{"ipa":"/klæːm/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[kleːm] ~ [klɛːm]"},{"ipa":"/klɛm/","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":240784,"upstream_offset":1195071685,"upstream_record_sha256":"6f6cd30e4e5aefe7ace2ce356dc579a9d4c09eb807b25e9133f63c16be8770b8","word":"clam"}
